Implement LP method details:

Function compute_score(a):
 if sum <0.01 return inf
 b=np.convolve(a,a)
 return 2*n*b.max()/sum(a)**2
But for speed, normalize sum=1, score = 2*n*b.max(). Need full convolve. For n up to 4096, np.convolve direct O(n²)=16M multiply-add maybe okay? np.convolve uses direct? It might be O(n²) in C but can be slower for 4096 (16M) fine; for 8192 67M maybe still okay. FFT could be faster but need ensure nonnegative and precision. We can use np.convolve for accuracy; n=4096 direct 16M ~0.02s? In numpy, convolution maybe O(n²) not FFT? np.convolve direct C? 16M float ~0.1s. OK. We also need LP matrix O(n²) bigger. n=4096 matrix 16M nonzeros, solving maybe seconds.

LP method:
- Precompute row_idx,col_idx for n (int32). total n². For n=4096, arrays length 16.7M each int32 -> 67MB each, data 134MB, total 270MB. Might be high. Use n up to 2048 for arrays 8MB each? Actually 4.2M -> 16MB each, data34MB total 66MB. Good. Could do n=3072 total 9.4M -> 75MB total. Use 2048 safe.

- Precompute once per n. To avoid repeated rows-cols memory for multiple n, maybe sequentially within each n and delete.

- Use f as float64 length n, sum=1.
- At each iteration:
   conv = np.convolve(f,f) maybe direct; M=conv.max()
   score=2*n*M (since sum1)
   update best if score<best_score.
   data = f[rows - cols]
   A = csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2*n-1, n))
   c = -np.ones(n)
   b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, M)
   res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0, None), method='highs')
   if failure break.
   g=res.x
   if g.sum() <=0: break
   g = g / g.sum()
   # Choose step: maybe line search to reduce score.
   base=f.copy(), best_t=0, best_f=f, best_local=score
   for t in [0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]? Evaluate score for candidate = (1-t)f+t*g clipped renorm. Pick best and decrease if not improve.
   But each score requires conv O(n²) direct. Could be okay for few t. Or simply t=0.5 and if score worsens reduce.
   Since LP is heuristic, do line search for robustness.

- To prevent oscillation and get convergence, maybe use t decreasing.
- Maintain best_f per n globally.

Implementation details:

`next_power_of_two(m)`: use 1 << (m-1).bit_length().

`convolve_nonnegative(a, n)`:
   if n <= 64: return np.convolve(a,a)
   nfft = next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
   ca = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:2*n-1]
   ca[ca < 0 and > small] =0
   return ca
But `fa*fa` not complex conjugate? For real a, FFT fa is complex; a*a coefficients are fa * fa (same, not conjugate) because product of same sequence. For convolution, FFT of a is F, product F*F. Yes no conjugate. Good. The result real? F*F may not have Hermitian symmetry? Since F*F: coefficient? The polynomial A(z)^2; DFT values F(k)^2. The inverse may be real if F has property? For real sequence, product of DFTs not necessarily Hermitian? Wait convolution of real a with real a is real. The DFT of convolution is F*F (pointwise). The inverse of F*F must be real if F*F satisfies conjugate symmetry: (F*F)[N-k]=conj(F[k])^2 = conj(F[k]^2). Yes because conj(F[N-k])=F[k]. So real. Use irfft.

Scoring:
```
def score_from_conv(a, conv):
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    return float(2*len(a)*conv.max()/ (s*s))
```
For internal, assume sum1 so score=2n*conv.max().

          if attempts>10 break
   return best

```

  w = softmax_weights(conv, M, rank)
  G = cross_corr_weights(a,w,nfft)
  G = np.maximum(G, 0)
  gmean = G.mean()
  d = G - gmean
  rng_ = rng.random? for jitter
  dmax = d.max() - d.min()
  if dmax < 1e-12:
     x += 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n); continue
  d = d / dmax  # in [-?,?]
  accepted=False
  t = base_step
  for _ in range(6):
      x_t = x - t*d
      a_t = softmax_vec(x_t)
      score_t = score_fast(a_t)
      if score_t < score - 1e-12:
          x=a? x=x_t; a=a_t; score=score_t; accepted=True; break
      t *= 0.5
  if accepted:
      base_step = min(2.0, base_step*1.25)
      stagn=0
  else:
      base_step *=0.5
      stagn +=1
      if base_step < 0.02 or stagn > 30:
          # restart

          if restarts > 20: break
```

`convolve_sequence(a, nfft=None)`:
```
n=len(a)
if n <= 64: return np.convolve(a,a,"full")
if nfft is None: nfft=get_nfft(n)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,nfft)[:2*n-1]
np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
return c
```
`cross_corr_weights(a,w,n,nfft)`:
```
revw = w[::-1]
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
fw=np.fft.rfft(revw,nfft)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,nfft)
G=c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
G=np.maximum(G,0,out=G)
return G
```

Initial x generation function:
```
def initial_x(n, rng, kind):
    if kind==0: x = rng.normal(0, 0.5, n)
    elif kind==1: x = rng.normal(0, 1.0, n)
    elif kind==2:
       freq = rng.uniform(0.5, 5.0)
       phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
       vals = 1.0 + 0.7*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*np.arange(n)/n + phase)
       vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.05)
       x = np.log(vals)
    elif kind==3:
       # chirp positive
       t=np.arange(n)
       vals=1+0.8*np.cos(np.pi*t*t/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       clip
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    x -= x.mean()
    return x
```
Ensure positive: if vals <=0, set 1e-6? Clipping to 0.05 for kind 2/3. For kind random normal, x can be negative large; softmax positive.

Initial a = softmax(x). Some may be very small; fine.

Pseudo for smooth_optimize:
```
def smooth_optimize(n, rng, local_deadline, best_in_score):
    best_a=None; best_local=best_in_score
    restarts=0; max_restarts=30
    nfft=get_nfft(n)
    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline and restarts<max_restarts:
       kind = restarts % 4
       x = initial_x(n,rng,kind)
       a=softmax_vec(x)
       step=0.8; frac=0.2; stagn=0; no_accept=0
       while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
          # if a<=0? 
          fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
          conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,nfft)[:2*n-1]
          np.maximum(conv,0,out=conv)
          M=conv.max(); score=2*n*M # sum=1
          if not finite: break
          if score < best_local:
              best_local=score; best_a=a.copy()
              if score <= lower+1e-8: return best_a,best_local
          if M <=1e-15: break
          # weights
          tau=frac*M
          if tau <=0: w=np.ones...
          else: z=(conv-M)/tau; w=np.exp(z); sw=w.sum(); if sw<=0: w uniform else w/=sw
          dw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],nfft)
          G=np.fft.irfft(fa*dw,nfft)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
          np.maximum(G,0,out=G)
          gmean=G.mean()
          d=G-gmean
          dmax=d.max()-d.min()
          if dmax < 1e-14:
             x = x + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n); x-=x.mean(); a=softmax_vec(x); stagn+=1; continue
          d=d/dmax
          t=step
          accepted=False
          for _ in range(5):
             x_t=x - t*d
             a_t=softmax_vec(x_t)
             # score candidate
             fa_t=np.fft.rfft(a_t,nfft)
             conv_t=np.fft.irfft(fa_t*fa_t,nfft)[:2*n-1]
             np.maximum(conv_t,0,out=conv_t)
             score_t=2*n*conv_t.max()
             if np.isfinite(score_t) and score_t < score + 1e-12: # maybe accept if not worse
                 x=x_t; a=a_t; score=score_t; accepted=True
                 if score_t < best_local: ...
                 break
             t*=0.5
          if accepted:
             step=min(2.0, step*1.1)
             no_accept=0
             if score < best_local: stagn=0
             else: stagn +=1
          else:
             no_accept +=1
             step=max(0.1, step*0.5)
             if no_accept > 8:
                # adapt frac/jitter
                frac = 0.05 + 0.8*rng.random()
                x = x + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n); x-=x.mean(); a=softmax_vec(x)
                no_accept=0; stagn+=1
             else:
                stagn +=1
          if stagn > 200:
             break
       restarts +=1
       # optional shuffle best back? maybe use best as next init?
       if best_a is not None and rng.random()<0.5:
           # use best as start for next restart with small perturbation? We'll do at top maybe.
    return best_a,best_local
```

Implement `compress_x(x)`:
```
def compress_x(x):
    r = x.max() - x.min()
    if r > 20.0 and r > 0:
        x = x.mean() + (x - x.mean()) * (20.0 / r)
    return x
```

def next_fft_len(n):
    m = 3*n - 2
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

`make_initial_x(n, rng, kind, base_x=None)`:
```
def make_initial_x(n, rng, kind, base_x):
    if base_x is not None and rng.random() < 0.75:
        x = base_x + 0.15*rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 0:
        x = rng.normal(0, 0.35, n)
    elif kind == 1:
        x = rng.normal(0, 1.0, n)
    elif kind == 2:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
        f = rng.uniform(0.25, 8.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
        v = 1.0 + 0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t/n + ph)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.05)
        x = np.log(v)
    elif kind == 3:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
        f = rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
        v = 1.0 + 0.8*np.cos(np.pi*f*t*t/n + ph)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.05)
        x = np.log(v)
    else:
        x = rng.normal(0, 0.7, n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if not np.isfinite(x).all(): x[~np.isfinite(x)]=0.0
    x = x - np.mean(x)
    return compress_x(x)
```

`smooth_optimize(n, rng, local_deadline, global_best_score=None)`:

def smooth_optimize(n, rng, local_deadline, global_best_score=None):
    best_a = None
    best_local = np.inf
    nfft = next_fft_len(n)
    restart = 0
    max_restarts = 8
    base_x = None
    while restart < max_restarts and time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
       if base_x is not None and rng.random()<0.6:
          x = make_initial_x(n,rng,0,base_x)
       else:
          x = make_initial_x(n,rng,restart % 4, None)
       a = softmax_vec(x)
       step = 0.8
       frac = 0.2
       flat = 0
       no_accept_streak = 0
       while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
          # current score
          fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
          conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:2*n-1]
          conv = np.maximum(conv, 0.0)
          M = float(np.max(conv))
          s = float(np.sum(a))
          if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0 or s < 0.01:
              a = softmax_vec(x + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n)); continue? Hmm
          score = 2.0*n*M/(s*s)
          if score < best_local:
              best_local = score; best_a = a.copy(); base_x = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)) - base log mean
          if best_local <= 2.0*n/(2.0*n-1) + 1e-7:
              break
          # weights
          tau = frac * M
          if tau <= 1e-300:
              w = np.ones(2*n-1, dtype=float)/(2*n-1)
          else:
              z = (conv - M) / tau
              z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
              # avoid underflow: if min < -745, exp 0 okay
              w = np.exp(z)
              sw = float(np.sum(w))
              if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0:
                  # find indices near max
                  w = np.zeros_like(conv); idx = np.where(conv >= M*(1-1e-6))[0]; w[idx]=1; sw=len(idx)
              w /= sw
          fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], nfft)
          G = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, nfft)
          G = G[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
          G = np.maximum(G, 0.0)
          d = G - G.mean()
          dmax = float(d.max() - d.min())
          if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-14:
              # jitter
              x = compress_x(x + 0.15*rng.standard_normal(n)); a=softmax_vec(x); flat+=1; no_accept_streak+=1
              if flat>20: break
              continue
          d = d / dmax
          t = step
          accepted = False
          for _ in range(6):
              x_t = compress_x(x - t*d)
              a_t = softmax_vec(x_t)
              s_t = float(np.sum(a_t))
              if s_t < 0.01:
                  t *= 0.5; continue
              # score
              fa_t = np.fft.rfft(a_t, nfft)
              conv_t = np.fft.irfft(fa_t*fa_t, nfft)[:2*n-1]
              conv_t = np.maximum(conv_t,0.0)
              score_t = 2.0*n*float(np.max(conv_t))/(s_t*s_t)
              if np.isfinite(score_t) and score_t < score - 1e-12:
                  x = x_t; a = a_t; score = score_t; accepted = True
                  if score < best_local:
                      best_local = score; best_a = a.copy(); base_x = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)) - base_x.mean? Need compute
                  break
              t *= 0.5
          if accepted:
              step = min(2.0, step*1.15)
              no_accept_streak=0
              if score >= best_local - 1e-10:
                  flat += 1
          else:
              no_accept_streak += 1
              flat += 1
              step = max(0.05, step*0.5)
              if no_accept_streak >= 6:
                  frac = 0.05 + 0.8*rng.random()
                  x = compress_x(x + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n))
                  a = softmax_vec(x)
                  no_accept_streak=0
              if flat > 80:
                  break
       restart += 1
       if best_a is None:
           base_x = None
    if best_a is None:
       return None, np.inf
    if global_best_score is not None and best_local >= global_best_score:
       return best_a, best_local
    return best_a, best_local
```

def polish_sequence(best, best_score, rng, deadline):
    n=len(best)
    if n <=0: return
    logbest=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
    for sigma in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3):
       while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
          cand=np.exp(logbest + sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
          s=cand.sum()

          sc=score_fast(cand)

             best=cand; best_score=sc; logbest=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
             if sc <= lower+1e-7: return
    return best,best_score

Let's write `score_fast` carefully for n large:
```
def score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    if n <= 128:
       c = np.convolve(a, a, mode='full')
    else:
       nfft = next_fft_len(n)
       fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
       c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:2*n-1]
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return float(2*n*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```

Let's ensure prefix generation stable:
```
def make_prefix_x(n, rng=None):
    p = np.empty(n)
    p[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
       p[i] = p[i-1] * (2*i -1)/(2*i)
    # Normalize not needed for x; x=log(p) mean subtract
    x = np.log(p)
    x -= np.mean(x)
    return compress_x(x)
```
For n large, loop O(n) fine (4096). Could vectorize but loop okay.

Add kind 4 (prefix), kind 5 (prefix with small random noise), kind 6 (prefix + tail boost): `x = log(p) + beta*t/n`? We can include `beta` random. But make_initial_x with kind 4.

start_kind = 4 if restart==0 else (restart%4)

Pseudo code refined:

```
def smooth_optimize(n, rng, local_deadline):
    best_a = None
    best_local = np.inf
    base_x = None
    nfft = next_fft_len(n)
    k = 0
    kinds = [4,5,0,2,1,3,1,2,3,0,4,5]
    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
       kind = kinds[k % len(kinds)]
       use_base = (best_a is not None and k > 1 and rng.random() < 0.4)
       if use_base:
          x = base_x + 0.15*rng.standard_normal(n)
          x = compress_x(x - x.mean())
       else:
          x = make_initial_x(n, rng, kind, None)
       a = softmax_vec(x)
       frac = 0.95 if kind in (4,5) or use_base else 0.2
       step = 0.8
       flat = 0
       noacc = 0
       while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
          # compute current fa/conv/score
          fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
          conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:2*n-1]
          conv = np.maximum(conv, 0.0)
          M = float(conv.max())
          if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M):
              break
          s = float(a.sum())
          if s < 0.01: break
          score = 2.0*n*M/(s*s)
          if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_local:
              best_local = score
              best_a = a.copy()
              base_x = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); base_x -= base_x.mean(); base_x = compress_x(base_x)
          if best_local <= 2.0*n/(2.0*n-1) + 1e-7:
              break
          # weights
          if frac >= 0.9:
             tol = 1e-8*M + 1e-12
             w = (conv >= M - tol).astype(float)
             sw = float(w.sum())
          else:
             tau = frac * M
             z = (conv - M)/tau
             z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
             w = np.exp(z)
             sw = float(w.sum())
             if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0:
                w = np.zeros_like(conv); w[int(np.argmax(conv))]=1; sw=1
          if sw <=0:
                w = np.zeros_like(conv); w[int(np.argmax(conv))]=1; sw=1
          w = w / sw
          fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], nfft)
          G = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, nfft)
          G = G[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
          G = np.maximum(G, 0.0)
          d = G - G.mean()
          dmax = float(d.max() - d.min())
          if dmax <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
              x = compress_x(x + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n) - 0)
              # ensure mean?
              x = x - x.mean()
              a = softmax_vec(x)
              flat += 1
              if flat > 30: break
              continue
          d = d / dmax
          t = step
          accepted = False
          for _ in range(5):
              x_t = compress_x(x - t*d)
              x_t = x_t - x_t.mean()
              a_t = softmax_vec(x_t)
              s_t = float(a_t.sum())
              if s_t < 0.01:
                  t *=0.5; continue
              fa_t = np.fft.rfft(a_t, nfft)
              conv_t = np.fft.irfft(fa_t*fa_t, nfft)[:2*n-1]
              conv_t = np.maximum(conv_t, 0.0)
              score_t = 2.0*n*float(conv_t.max())/(s_t*s_t)
              if np.isfinite(score_t) and score_t < score - 1e-12:
                  x = x_t; a = a_t; score = score_t; accepted = True
                  if score < best_local:
                      best_local = score; best_a = a.copy(); base_x=...
                  break
              t *=0.5
          if accepted:
              step = min(2.0, step*1.1)
              noacc=0
              if score >= best_local - 1e-10:
                  flat +=1
          else:
              noacc +=1; flat +=1
              step=max(0.05, step*0.5)
              if noacc >=5:
                  frac_choices = [0.05,0.15,0.3,0.6,0.95]
                  frac = frac_choices[int(rng.integers(0,len(frac_choices)))]
                  x = compress_x(x + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n))
                  x = x - x.mean()
                  a=softmax_vec(x)
                  noacc=0
          if flat > 150:
              break
       k += 1
       if k > 100: break
    return best_a,best_local
```

Note: `x = compress_x(x + 0.1*rng... )` then `x = x - x.mean()`; if compress after mean, okay. Maybe define helper `randomize_x(x, rng, sigma)`.

Potential issue: active mask with tol includes many lags if b values broad near max; okay. If active set too many (almost all), G maybe weak. But frac=0.95 only initial prefix; when noacc changes to other fracs.

Now, `parametric_search`:
```
def parametric_search(rng, deadline, best_a, best_score):
    seqs_consider = []
    # prefix sequences for n 256,1024,4096
    for n in [256,1024,4096]:
       a=make_prefix_sequence(n)
       sc=score_fast(a)
       update
    # maybe reversed prefix same
    for n in [256,1024,4096]:
       x = np.arange(n); x/=max(1,n-1)
       for beta in [0,0.2,0.5,1,2,3,5,8]:
          for gamma in [0,0.5,2]:
             u=(1+beta*x+gamma*x*x)/np.sqrt(np.arange(n)+0.5)
             if not finite: continue
             a=u/u.sum(); sc=score_fast(a)
             update
    return best_a,best_score
```

Another possible exact approach is to use the fact that for a fixed active set at the peak, stationarity gives a shifted-average condition. We can implement an active-set fixed point: choose active lags where b is near max, compute G (shifted average), and set a proportional to something that equalizes G: x_i -= eta(G_i-mean) (which is exactly what we already have). ```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The method uses:
  * a deterministic central-binomial "flat" fallback,
  * a small parametric family of left-singular weighted sequences,
  * a log-simplex multiplicative gradient / active-set search with FFT-backed
    convolution and gradient computation.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

def next_fft_len(n):
    """FFT length sufficient for both a*a and cross-correlated sums."""
    m = 3 * n - 2
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def softmax_vec(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if not np.isfinite(x).all():
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / x.size)
    return e / s

def compress_x(x, range_limit=22.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    if not np.isfinite(x).all():
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    mn = float(np.min(x))
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    r = mx - mn
    if r > range_limit and r > 0.0:
        x = float(np.mean(x)) + (x - float(np.mean(x))) * (range_limit / r)
    return x - float(np.mean(x))

def score_fast(a):
    """Fast internal score; equivalent to evaluate_sequence for valid data."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    if n <= 96:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        L = next_fft_len(n)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))

def make_prefix_sequence(n):
    """Exact finite prefix of (1-z)^(-1/2), normalized to sum 1."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    p[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return p / s

def make_initial_x(n, rng, kind, base_x=None):
    n = int(n)
    if base_x is not None:
        x = base_x + 0.18 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        return compress_x(x)

    if kind == 4:  # central-binomial prefix plus tiny noise
        p = make_prefix_sequence(n)
        x = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
        x += 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 5:  # reversed prefix plus tiny noise
        p = make_prefix_sequence(n)
        x = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
        x = x[::-1] + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 0:  # near-uniform random phase
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n)
    elif kind == 1:  # wider random
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    elif kind == 2:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
        f = rng.uniform(0.25, 8.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        vals = 1.0 + 0.85 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t / max(1, n) + ph)
        vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.05)
        x = np.log(vals)
    elif kind == 3:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
        f = rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        vals = 1.0 + 0.85 * np.cos(np.pi * f * t * t / max(1, n) + ph)
        vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.05)
        x = np.log(vals)
    else:
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, n)

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    return compress_x(x)

def parametric_search(rng, deadline, best_a, best_score):
    """Evaluate a small family of structured sequences to seed the search."""
    while True:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        stop = False
        for n in (256, 1024, 2048, 4096):
            if stop:
                break
            idx = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
            x = idx / max(1, n - 1)
            base = np.sqrt(idx + 0.5)
            rhs_base = np.sqrt(np.maximum(n - 1 - idx, 0.5))

            for beta in (-0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                for gamma in (0.0, 0.5, 1.5):
                    for delta in (0.0, 0.5, 1.5):
                        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                            stop = True
                            break
                        u = (1.0 + beta * x + gamma * x * x) / base
                        if delta != 0.0:
                            u = u + delta / rhs_base
                        u = np.maximum(u, 1e-8)
                        s = float(np.sum(u))
                        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                            continue
                        a = u / s
                        sc = score_fast(a)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                            best_score = sc
                            best_a = a.copy()
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
            break
    return best_a, best_score

def smooth_optimize(n, rng, local_deadline):
    """Local/global log-simplex search for a fixed length n."""
    n = int(n)
    best_a = None
    best_local = np.inf
    base_x = None
    L = next_fft_len(n)

    kinds = [4, 5, 0, 2, 1, 3, 1, 2, 0, 3, 4, 5]
    k = 0

    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline and k < 120:
        kind = kinds[k % len(kinds)]
        use_base = best_a is not None and k > 1 and rng.random() < 0.35
        if use_base:
            x = base_x + 0.16 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            x = compress_x(x)
        else:
            x = make_initial_x(n, rng, kind, None)

        a = softmax_vec(x)
        frac = 0.05 if kind in (4, 5) or use_base else 0.2
        step = 0.9
        flat = 0
        no_accept = 0

        while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
            fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
            conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
            conv = np.maximum(conv, 0.0)
            M = float(np.max(conv))
            if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
                break
            s = float(np.sum(a))
            if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
                break
            score = 2.0 * n * M / (s * s)
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                break
            if score < best_local:
                best_local = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                base_x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                base_x = compress_x(base_x)

            # The theoretical full-convolution lower bound for this length.
            if best_local <= 2.0 * n / (2.0 * n - 1.0) + 1e-7:
                break

            # Build weights on convolution lags.
            if frac >= 0.9:
                tol = 1e-10 * M + 1e-12
                w = (conv >= M - tol).astype(float)
                sw = float(np.sum(w))
            else:
                tau = max(1e-300, frac * M)
                z = (conv - M) / tau
                z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
                w = np.exp(z)
                sw = float(np.sum(w))
                if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
                    w = np.zeros_like(conv)
                    w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
                    sw = 1.0
            if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
                w = np.zeros_like(conv)
                w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
                sw = 1.0
            w = w / sw

            # G_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}, i on support.
            fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
            G = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, L)
            G = G[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
            G = np.maximum(G, 0.0)

            d = G - float(np.mean(G))
            dmax = float(np.max(d) - np.min(d))
            if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-14:
                x = compress_x(x + 0.12 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                a = softmax_vec(x)
                flat += 1
                if flat > 35:
                    break
                continue

            d = d / dmax
            t = step
            accepted = False
            for _ in range(5):
                x_t = compress_x(x - t * d)
                a_t = softmax_vec(x_t)
                s_t = float(np.sum(a_t))
                if s_t < 0.01:
                    t *= 0.5
                    continue
                fa_t = np.fft.rfft(a_t, L)
                conv_t = np.fft.irfft(fa_t * fa_t, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
                conv_t = np.maximum(conv_t, 0.0)
                score_t = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(conv_t)) / (s_t * s_t)
                if np.isfinite(score_t) and score_t < score - 1e-12:
                    x = x_t
                    a = a_t
                    score = score_t
                    accepted = True
                    if score < best_local:
                        best_local = score
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        base_x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                        base_x = compress_x(base_x)
                    break
                t *= 0.5

            if accepted:
                step = min(2.0, step * 1.12)
                no_accept = 0
                if score >= best_local - 1e-10:
                    flat += 1
            else:
                no_accept += 1
                flat += 1
                step = max(0.05, step * 0.5)
                if no_accept >= 5:
                    choices = (0.02, 0.05, 0.12, 0.3, 0.7, 0.95)
                    frac = choices[int(rng.integers(0, len(choices)))]
                    x = compress_x(x + 0.22 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                    a = softmax_vec(x)
                    no_accept = 0

            if flat > 160:
                break
        k += 1

    return best_a, best_local

def polish_sequence(a, score, rng, deadline):
    """Final stochastic local polish around the current best sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    n = int(a.size)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    loga = compress_x(loga)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    for sigma in (0.03, 0.06, 0.12, 0.25):
        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
            cand = softmax_vec(loga + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))
            sc = score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
                loga = compress_x(np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300)))
    return best_a, best_score

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800.0, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(5.0, float(budget_s) - 6.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Safe fallback: central-binomial prefix near n=1024.
    best_a = make_prefix_sequence(1024)
    best_score = score_fast(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(32, dtype=float)
        best_score = 2.0

    # If a previously found sequence is present, try it once.
    try:
        base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
        base = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if base.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(base)):
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(base.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = base.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Parametric seed search.
    param_deadline = min(deadline - 20.0, start + 90.0)
    best_a, best_score = parametric_search(rng, param_deadline, best_a, best_score)

    # Length schedule. Larger lengths are given the bulk of remaining time.
    schedule = [
        (64, 8),
        (128, 15),
        (256, 35),
        (512, 90),
        (1024, 220),
        (2048, 460),
        (4096, -1),
    ]

    for n, alloc in schedule:
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now
        if remaining < 15.0:
            break
        if alloc < 0:
            alloc = remaining - 10.0
        else:
            alloc = min(float(alloc), remaining - 10.0)
        if alloc < 5.0:
            break
        local_deadline = now + alloc
        try:
            a, sc = smooth_optimize(n, rng, local_deadline)
        except Exception:
            a, sc = None, np.inf
        if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = sc

    # Final polish with any remaining time.
    polish_deadline = deadline - 1.0
    if time.monotonic() < polish_deadline and best_a is not None:
        a, sc = polish_sequence(best_a, best_score, rng, polish_deadline)
        if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = sc

    # Ensure a valid return.
    if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = make_prefix_sequence(512)
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=float)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(best_a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return [float(1.0)] * 32
    best_a = best_a / s
    return [float(v) for v in best_a]
```
